<content lines="1-246">
  1 | # Design System Inspired by Spotify
  2 | 
  3 | ## 1. Visual Theme & Atmosphere
  4 | 
  5 | Spotify's web interface is a dark, immersive music player that wraps listeners in a near-black cocoon (`#121212`, `#181818`, `#1f1f1f`) where album art and content become the primary source of color. The design philosophy is "content-first darkness" — the UI recedes into shadow so that music, podcasts, and playlists can glow. Every surface is a shade of charcoal, creating a theater-like environment where the only true color comes from the iconic Spotify Green (`#1ed760`) and the album artwork itself.
  6 | 
  7 | The typography uses SpotifyMixUI and SpotifyMixUITitle — proprietary fonts from the CircularSp family (Circular by Lineto, customized for Spotify) with an extensive fallback stack that includes Arabic, Hebrew, Cyrillic, Greek, Devanagari, and CJK fonts, reflecting Spotify's global reach. The type system is compact and functional: 700 (bold) for emphasis and navigation, 600 (semibold) for secondary emphasis, and 400 (regular) for body. Buttons use uppercase with positive letter-spacing (1.4px–2px) for a systematic, label-like quality.
  8 | 
  9 | What distinguishes Spotify is its pill-and-circle geometry. Primary buttons use 500px–9999px radius (full pill), circular play buttons use 50% radius, and search inputs are 500px pills. Combined with heavy shadows (`rgba(0,0,0,0.5) 0px 8px 24px`) on elevated elements and a unique inset border-shadow combo (`rgb(18,18,18) 0px 1px 0px, rgb(124,124,124) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset`), the result is an interface that feels like a premium audio device — tactile, rounded, and built for touch.
 10 | 
 11 | **Key Characteristics:**
 12 | - Near-black immersive dark theme (`#121212`–`#1f1f1f`) — UI disappears behind content
 13 | - Spotify Green (`#1ed760`) as singular brand accent — never decorative, always functional
 14 | - SpotifyMixUI/CircularSp font family with global script support
 15 | - Pill buttons (500px–9999px) and circular controls (50%) — rounded, touch-optimized
 16 | - Uppercase button labels with wide letter-spacing (1.4px–2px)
 17 | - Heavy shadows on elevated elements (`rgba(0,0,0,0.5) 0px 8px 24px`)
 18 | - Semantic colors: negative red (`#f3727f`), warning orange (`#ffa42b`), announcement blue (`#539df5`)
 19 | - Album art as the primary color source — the UI is achromatic by design
 20 | 
 21 | ## 2. Color Palette & Roles
 22 | 
 23 | ### Primary Brand
 24 | - **Spotify Green** (`#1ed760`): Primary brand accent — play buttons, active states, CTAs
 25 | - **Near Black** (`#121212`): Deepest background surface
 26 | - **Dark Surface** (`#181818`): Cards, containers, elevated surfaces
 27 | - **Mid Dark** (`#1f1f1f`): Button backgrounds, interactive surfaces
 28 | 
 29 | ### Text
 30 | - **White** (`#ffffff`): `--text-base`, primary text
 31 | - **Silver** (`#b3b3b3`): Secondary text, muted labels, inactive nav
 32 | - **Near White** (`#cbcbcb`): Slightly brighter secondary text
 33 | - **Light** (`#fdfdfd`): Near-pure white for maximum emphasis
 34 | 
 35 | ### Semantic
 36 | - **Negative Red** (`#f3727f`): `--text-negative`, error states
 37 | - **Warning Orange** (`#ffa42b`): `--text-warning`, warning states
 38 | - **Announcement Blue** (`#539df5`): `--text-announcement`, info states
 39 | 
 40 | ### Surface & Border
 41 | - **Dark Card** (`#252525`): Elevated card surface
 42 | - **Mid Card** (`#272727`): Alternate card surface
 43 | - **Border Gray** (`#4d4d4d`): Button borders on dark
 44 | - **Light Border** (`#7c7c7c`): Outlined button borders, muted links
 45 | - **Separator** (`#b3b3b3`): Divider lines
 46 | - **Light Surface** (`#eeeeee`): Light-mode buttons (rare)
 47 | - **Spotify Green Border** (`#1db954`): Green accent border variant
 48 | 
 49 | ### Shadows
 50 | - **Heavy** (`rgba(0,0,0,0.5) 0px 8px 24px`): Dialogs, menus, elevated panels
 51 | - **Medium** (`rgba(0,0,0,0.3) 0px 8px 8px`): Cards, dropdowns
 52 | - **Inset Border** (`rgb(18,18,18) 0px 1px 0px, rgb(124,124,124) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset`): Input border-shadow combo
 53 | 
 54 | ## 3. Typography Rules
 55 | 
 56 | ### Font Families
 57 | - **Title**: `SpotifyMixUITitle`, fallbacks: `CircularSp-Arab, CircularSp-Hebr, CircularSp-Cyrl, CircularSp-Grek, CircularSp-Deva, Helvetica Neue, helvetica, arial, Hiragino Sans, Hiragino Kaku Gothic ProN, Meiryo, MS Gothic`
 58 | - **UI / Body**: `SpotifyMixUI`, same fallback stack
 59 | 
 60 | ### Hierarchy
 61 | 
 62 | | Role | Font | Size | Weight | Line Height | Letter Spacing | Notes |
 63 | |------|------|------|--------|-------------|----------------|-------|
 64 | | Section Title | SpotifyMixUITitle | 24px (1.50rem) | 700 | normal | normal | Bold title weight |
 65 | | Feature Heading | SpotifyMixUI | 18px (1.13rem) | 600 | 1.30 (tight) | normal | Semibold section heads |
 66 | | Body Bold | SpotifyMixUI | 16px (1.00rem) | 700 | normal | normal | Emphasized text |
 67 | | Body | SpotifyMixUI | 16px (1.00rem) | 400 | normal | normal | Standard body |
 68 | | Button Uppercase | SpotifyMixUI | 14px (0.88rem) | 600–700 | 1.00 (tight) | 1.4px–2px | `text-transform: uppercase` |
 69 | | Button | SpotifyMixUI | 14px (0.88rem) | 700 | normal | 0.14px | Standard button |
 70 | | Nav Link Bold | SpotifyMixUI | 14px (0.88rem) | 700 | normal | normal | Navigation |
 71 | | Nav Link | SpotifyMixUI | 14px (0.88rem) | 400 | normal | normal | Inactive nav |
 72 | | Caption Bold | SpotifyMixUI | 14px (0.88rem) | 700 | 1.50–1.54 | normal | Bold metadata |
 73 | | Caption | SpotifyMixUI | 14px (0.88rem) | 400 | normal | normal | Metadata |
 74 | | Small Bold | SpotifyMixUI | 12px (0.75rem) | 700 | 1.50 | normal | Tags, counts |
 75 | | Small | SpotifyMixUI | 12px (0.75rem) | 400 | normal | normal | Fine print |
 76 | | Badge | SpotifyMixUI | 10.5px (0.66rem) | 600 | 1.33 | normal | `text-transform: capitalize` |
 77 | | Micro | SpotifyMixUI | 10px (0.63rem) | 400 | normal | normal | Smallest text |
 78 | 
 79 | ### Principles
 80 | - **Bold/regular binary**: Most text is either 700 (bold) or 400 (regular), with 600 used sparingly. This creates a clear visual hierarchy through weight contrast rather than size variation.
 81 | - **Uppercase buttons as system**: Button labels use uppercase + wide letter-spacing (1.4px–2px), creating a systematic "label" voice distinct from content text.
 82 | - **Compact sizing**: The range is 10px–24px — narrower than most systems. Spotify's type is compact and functional, designed for scanning playlists, not reading articles.
 83 | - **Global script support**: The extensive fallback stack (Arabic, Hebrew, Cyrillic, Greek, Devanagari, CJK) reflects Spotify's 180+ market reach.
 84 | 
 85 | ## 4. Component Stylings
 86 | 
 87 | ### Buttons
 88 | 
 89 | **Dark Pill**
 90 | - Background: `#1f1f1f`
 91 | - Text: `#ffffff` or `#b3b3b3`
 92 | - Padding: 8px 16px
 93 | - Radius: 9999px (full pill)
 94 | - Use: Navigation pills, secondary actions
 95 | 
 96 | **Dark Large Pill**
 97 | - Background: `#181818`
 98 | - Text: `#ffffff`
 99 | - Padding: 0px 43px
100 | - Radius: 500px
101 | - Use: Primary app navigation buttons
102 | 
103 | **Light Pill**
104 | - Background: `#eeeeee`
105 | - Text: `#181818`
106 | - Radius: 500px
107 | - Use: Light-mode CTAs (cookie consent, marketing)
108 | 
109 | **Outlined Pill**
110 | - Background: transparent
111 | - Text: `#ffffff`
112 | - Border: `1px solid #7c7c7c`
113 | - Padding: 4px 16px 4px 36px (asymmetric for icon)
114 | - Radius: 9999px
115 | - Use: Follow buttons, secondary actions
116 | 
117 | **Circular Play**
118 | - Background: `#1f1f1f`
119 | - Text: `#ffffff`
120 | - Padding: 12px
121 | - Radius: 50% (circle)
122 | - Use: Play/pause controls
123 | 
124 | ### Cards & Containers
125 | - Background: `#181818` or `#1f1f1f`
126 | - Radius: 6px–8px
127 | - No visible borders on most cards
128 | - Hover: slight background lightening
129 | - Shadow: `rgba(0,0,0,0.3) 0px 8px 8px` on elevated
130 | 
131 | ### Inputs
132 | - Search input: `#1f1f1f` background, `#ffffff` text
133 | - Radius: 500px (pill)
134 | - Padding: 12px 96px 12px 48px (icon-aware)
135 | - Focus: border becomes `#000000`, outline `1px solid`
136 | 
137 | ### Navigation
138 | - Dark sidebar with SpotifyMixUI 14px weight 700 for active, 400 for inactive
139 | - `#b3b3b3` muted color for inactive items, `#ffffff` for active
140 | - Circular icon buttons (50% radius)
141 | - Spotify logo top-left in green
142 | 
143 | ## 5. Layout Principles
144 | 
145 | ### Spacing System
146 | - Base unit: 8px
147 | - Scale: 1px, 2px, 3px, 4px, 5px, 6px, 8px, 10px, 12px, 14px, 15px, 16px, 20px
148 | 
149 | ### Grid & Container
150 | - Sidebar (fixed) + main content area
151 | - Grid-based album/playlist cards
152 | - Full-width now-playing bar at bottom
153 | - Responsive content area fills remaining space
154 | 
155 | ### Whitespace Philosophy
156 | - **Dark compression**: Spotify packs content densely — playlist grids, track lists, and navigation are all tightly spaced. The dark background provides visual rest between elements without needing large gaps.
157 | - **Content density over breathing room**: This is an app, not a marketing site. Every pixel serves the listening experience.
158 | 
159 | ### Border Radius Scale
160 | - Minimal (2px): Badges, explicit tags
161 | - Subtle (4px): Inputs, small elements
162 | - Standard (6px): Album art containers, cards
163 | - Comfortable (8px): Sections, dialogs
164 | - Medium (10px–20px): Panels, overlay elements
165 | - Large (100px): Large pill buttons
166 | - Pill (500px): Primary buttons, search input
167 | - Full Pill (9999px): Navigation pills, search
168 | - Circle (50%): Play buttons, avatars, icons
169 | 
170 | ## 6. Depth & Elevation
171 | 
172 | | Level | Treatment | Use |
173 | |-------|-----------|-----|
174 | | Base (Level 0) | `#121212` background | Deepest layer, page background |
175 | | Surface (Level 1) | `#181818` or `#1f1f1f` | Cards, sidebar, containers |
176 | | Elevated (Level 2) | `rgba(0,0,0,0.3) 0px 8px 8px` | Dropdown menus, hover cards |
177 | | Dialog (Level 3) | `rgba(0,0,0,0.5) 0px 8px 24px` | Modals, overlays, menus |
178 | | Inset (Border) | `rgb(18,18,18) 0px 1px 0px, rgb(124,124,124) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset` | Input borders |
179 | 
180 | **Shadow Philosophy**: Spotify uses notably heavy shadows for a dark-themed app. The 0.5 opacity shadow at 24px blur creates a dramatic "floating in darkness" effect for dialogs and menus, while the 0.3 opacity at 8px blur provides a more subtle card lift. The unique inset border-shadow combination on inputs creates a recessed, tactile quality.
181 | 
182 | ## 7. Do's and Don'ts
183 | 
184 | ### Do
185 | - Use near-black backgrounds (`#121212`–`#1f1f1f`) — depth through shade variation
186 | - Apply Spotify Green (`#1ed760`) only for play controls, active states, and primary CTAs
187 | - Use pill shape (500px–9999px) for all buttons — circular (50%) for play controls
188 | - Apply uppercase + wide letter-spacing (1.4px–2px) on button labels
189 | - Keep typography compact (10px–24px range) — this is an app, not a magazine
190 | - Use heavy shadows (`0.3–0.5 opacity`) for elevated elements on dark backgrounds
191 | - Let album art provide color — the UI itself is achromatic
192 | 
193 | ### Don't
194 | - Don't use Spotify Green decoratively or on backgrounds — it's functional only
195 | - Don't use light backgrounds for primary surfaces — the dark immersion is core
196 | - Don't skip the pill/circle geometry on buttons — square buttons break the identity
197 | - Don't use thin/subtle shadows — on dark backgrounds, shadows need to be heavy to be visible
198 | - Don't add additional brand colors — green + achromatic grays is the complete palette
199 | - Don't use relaxed line-heights — Spotify's typography is compact and dense
200 | - Don't expose raw gray borders — use shadow-based or inset borders instead
201 | 
202 | ## 8. Responsive Behavior
203 | 
204 | ### Breakpoints
205 | | Name | Width | Key Changes |
206 | |------|-------|-------------|
207 | | Mobile Small | <425px | Compact mobile layout |
208 | | Mobile | 425–576px | Standard mobile |
209 | | Tablet | 576–768px | 2-column grid |
210 | | Tablet Large | 768–896px | Expanded layout |
211 | | Desktop Small | 896–1024px | Sidebar visible |
212 | | Desktop | 1024–1280px | Full desktop layout |
213 | | Large Desktop | >1280px | Expanded grid |
214 | 
215 | ### Collapsing Strategy
216 | - Sidebar: full → collapsed → hidden
217 | - Album grid: 5 columns → 3 → 2 → 1
218 | - Now-playing bar: maintained at all sizes
219 | - Search: pill input maintained, width adjusts
220 | - Navigation: sidebar → bottom bar on mobile
221 | 
222 | ## 9. Agent Prompt Guide
223 | 
224 | ### Quick Color Reference
225 | - Background: Near Black (`#121212`)
226 | - Surface: Dark Card (`#181818`)
227 | - Text: White (`#ffffff`)
228 | - Secondary text: Silver (`#b3b3b3`)
229 | - Accent: Spotify Green (`#1ed760`)
230 | - Border: `#4d4d4d`
231 | - Error: Negative Red (`#f3727f`)
232 | 
233 | ### Example Component Prompts
234 | - "Create a dark card: #181818 background, 8px radius. Title at 16px SpotifyMixUI weight 700, white text. Subtitle at 14px weight 400, #b3b3b3. Shadow rgba(0,0,0,0.3) 0px 8px 8px on hover."
235 | - "Design a pill button: #1f1f1f background, white text, 9999px radius, 8px 16px padding. 14px SpotifyMixUI weight 700, uppercase, letter-spacing 1.4px."
236 | - "Build a circular play button: Spotify Green (#1ed760) background, #000000 icon, 50% radius, 12px padding."
237 | - "Create search input: #1f1f1f background, white text, 500px radius, 12px 48px padding. Inset border: rgb(124,124,124) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset."
238 | - "Design navigation sidebar: #121212 background. Active items: 14px weight 700, white. Inactive: 14px weight 400, #b3b3b3."
239 | 
240 | ### Iteration Guide
241 | 1. Start with #121212 — everything lives in near-black darkness
242 | 2. Spotify Green for functional highlights only (play, active, CTA)
243 | 3. Pill everything — 500px for large, 9999px for small, 50% for circular
244 | 4. Uppercase + wide tracking on buttons — the systematic label voice
245 | 5. Heavy shadows (0.3–0.5 opacity) for elevation — light shadows are invisible on dark
246 | 6. Album art provides all the color — the UI stays achromatic
</content>

  2 | import SummaryCard from "../components/SummaryCard";
  3 | import { SBARBrief } from "../components";
  4 | import { AgentReasoningStream } from "../agent-reasoning";
  5 | 
  6 | // Mock data for the "Money View"
  7 | const mockSBAR = {
  8 |   risk: "RED" as const,
  9 |   patientId: "P-101",
 10 |   stayCount: "Day 4",
 11 |   window: "Last 6h as of 08:15",
 12 |   trend: "Progressive respiratory fatigue; rate increased from 18 to 28 over 4h.",
 13 |   conflict: "Lactate is rising (4.5) despite stable Blood Pressure, suggesting occult shock.",
 14 |   timeBomb: "High risk of intubation requirement within 120 minutes.",
 15 |   reasoning: [
 16 |     "HR: 112bpm (High > 110)",
 17 |     "SpO2: 91% (Low oxygen saturation)",
 18 |     "Lactate: 4.5 mmol/L (Rising)",
 19 |     "Patient appearing restless/combative"
 20 |   ]
 21 | };
 22 | 
 23 | const counts = [
 24 |   { label: "Critical Patients", value: 2 },
 25 |   { label: "Active Alerts", value: 5 },
 26 |   { label: "Pending Reviews", value: 3 },
 27 |   { label: "System Uptime", value: 99 },
 28 | ];
 29 | 
 30 | export default function Dashboard() {
 31 |   const [showAgentStream, setShowAgentStream] = useState(false);
 32 | 
 33 |   return (
 34 |     <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row gap-4 md:gap-6 animate-in fade-in duration-700 h-full">
 35 |       
 36 |       {/* LEFT COLUMN: Overview & Stats - Responsive width management */}
 37 |       <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-4 md:space-y-6 overflow-y-auto pb-4">
 38 |         
 39 |         {/* Header Panel - Responsive padding */}
 40 |         <header className="bg-bg-panel border border-border-subtle p-4 md:p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
 41 |           <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 42 |             <div>
 43 |               <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-text-primary">
 44 |                 Clinical Command Center
 45 |               </h2>
 46 |               <p className="text-text-secondary text-xs md:text-sm mt-1">
 47 |                 ICU Silent Deterioration Spotter • Unit North
 48 |               </p>
 49 |             </div>
 50 |             <button
 51 |               onClick={() => setShowAgentStream(!showAgentStream)}
 52 |               className="px-3 md:px-4 py-2 bg-accent hover:bg-accent/80 rounded-lg text-xs md:text-sm font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
 53 |             >
 54 |               {showAgentStream ? (
 55 |                 <>
 56 |                   <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
 57 |                     <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13.875 18.825A10.05 10.05 0 0112 19c-4.478 0-8.268-2.943-9.543-7a9.97 9.97 0 011.563-3.029m5.858.908a3 3 0 114.243 4.243M9.878 9.878l4.242 4.242M9.88 9.88l-3.29-3.29m7.532 7.532l3.29 3.29M3 3l3.59 3.59m0 0A9.953 9.953 0 0112 5c4.478 0 8.268 2.943 9.543 7a10.025 10.025 0 01-4.132 5.411m0 0L21 21" />
 58 |                   </svg>
 59 |                   Hide Agents
 60 |                 </>
 61 |               ) : (
 62 |                 <>
 63 |                   <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
 64 |                     <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15 12a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z" />
 65 |                     <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M2.458 12C3.732 7.943 7.523 5 12 5c4.478 0 8.268 2.943 9.542 7-1.274 4.057-5.064 7-9.542 7-4.477 0-8.268-2.943-9.542-7z" />
 66 |                   </svg>
 67 |                   Show Agents
 68 |                 </>
 69 |               )}
 70 |             </button>
 71 |           </div>
 72 |         </header>
 73 | 
 74 |         {/* Stats Grid - Responsive columns: 1 on mobile, 2 on tablet, 4 on desktop */}
 75 |         <section className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4">
 76 |           {counts.map((item) => (
 77 |             <SummaryCard
 78 |               key={item.label}
 79 |               label={item.label}
 80 |               value={item.value}
 81 |             />
 82 |           ))}
 83 |         </section>
 84 | 
 85 |         {/* Agent Reasoning Stream - Collapsible */}
 86 |         {showAgentStream && (
 87 |           <section className="animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top duration-500">
 88 |             <AgentReasoningStream patientId="10006" />
 89 |           </section>
 90 |         )}
 91 | 
 92 |         {/* Placeholder for Main Data / Charts - Responsive height */}
 93 |         {!showAgentStream && (
 94 |           <div className="bg-bg-panel border border-border-subtle rounded-xl p-4 md:p-6 h-64 md:h-80 xl:h-96 flex items-center justify-center border-dashed">
 95 |             <p className="text-text-muted italic text-sm md:text-base text-center px-4">
 96 |               Vitals Trend Visualization (Coming Soon)
 97 |             </p>
 98 |           </div>
 99 |         )}
100 |       </div>
101 | 
102 |       {/* RIGHT COLUMN: SBAR Brief - Responsive width and positioning */}
103 |       <aside className="w-full xl:w-[420px] 2xl:w-[480px] shrink-0">
104 |         {/* Sticky positioning only on large screens, full height on mobile */}
105 |         <div className="xl:sticky xl:top-0 h-[600px] md:h-[700px] xl:h-[calc(100vh-8rem)]">
106 |           <SBARBrief data={mockSBAR} />
107 |         </div>
108 |       </aside>
109 | 
110 |     </div>
111 |   );
112 | }
</content>

<file><path>FE/src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-138">
  1 | import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
  2 | 
  3 | type RiskLevel = "RED" | "YELLOW" | "GREEN";
  4 | 
  5 | interface Patient {
  6 |   id: string;
  7 |   name: string;
  8 |   bed: string;
  9 |   risk: RiskLevel;
 10 |   lastUpdate: string;
 11 |   alerts: string[];
 12 | }
 13 | 
 14 | export default function Sidebar() {
 15 |   const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>("101");
 16 |   const [loading] = useState(false);
 17 | 
 18 |   const [patients] = useState<Patient[]>([
 19 |     { id: "P-105", name: "Sarah Jenkins", bed: "04", risk: "YELLOW", lastUpdate: "2m ago", alerts: ["SpO2 Drop"] },
 20 |     { id: "P-101", name: "John Doe", bed: "01", risk: "RED", lastUpdate: "Just now", alerts: ["Tachycardia", "BP Labile"] },
 21 |     { id: "P-103", name: "Robert Kane", bed: "12", risk: "GREEN", lastUpdate: "15m ago", alerts: [] },
 22 |     { id: "P-102", name: "Jane Smith", bed: "02", risk: "RED", lastUpdate: "1m ago", alerts: ["Sepsis Suspected"] },
 23 |   ]);
 24 | 
 25 |   const sortedPatients = useMemo(() => {
 26 |     const riskOrder: Record<RiskLevel, number> = { RED: 0, YELLOW: 1, GREEN: 2 };
 27 |     return [...patients].sort((a, b) => riskOrder[a.risk] - riskOrder[b.risk]);
 28 |   }, [patients]);
 29 | 
 30 |   return (
 31 |     <aside className="w-80 bg-bg-panel border-r border-border-subtle flex flex-col h-screen shrink-0 shadow-2xl">
 32 |       {/* HEADER: Focus on System Integrity */}
 33 |       <div className="p-6 pb-4">
 34 |         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
 35 |           <h2 className="font-bold text-xs tracking-[0.2em] text-text-secondary uppercase">
 36 |             Unit: ICU-North
 37 |           </h2>
 38 |           <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
 39 |             <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-success"></span>
 40 |             <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-success uppercase">Live</span>
 41 |           </div>
 42 |         </div>
 43 |         <h1 className="text-xl font-black text-text-primary tracking-tight">
 44 |           COHORT <span className="text-text-muted font-light">ANALYSIS</span>
 45 |         </h1>
 46 |       </div>
 47 | 
 48 |       {/* FILTER TABS: Quick triage */}
 49 |       <div className="flex px-6 gap-4 border-b border-border-subtle pb-4">
 50 |          <button className="text-[10px] font-bold text-accent border-b border-accent pb-1">ALL ({patients.length})</button>
 51 |          <button className="text-[10px] font-bold text-text-muted hover:text-critical transition-colors">CRITICAL (2)</button>
 52 |       </div>
 53 | 
 54 |       {/* PATIENT LIST */}
 55 |       <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 space-y-2 custom-scrollbar">
 56 |         {loading ? (
 57 |            /* Loading State */
 58 |            <div className="space-y-3 px-3">
 59 |               {[1, 2, 3, 4].map(i => (
 60 |                 <div key={i} className="h-20 bg-bg-elevated/50 animate-pulse rounded-xl" />
 61 |               ))}
 62 |            </div>
 63 |         ) : (
 64 |           sortedPatients.map((patient) => {
 65 |             const isSelected = selectedId === patient.id;
 66 |             const riskColors = {
 67 |               RED: "border-l-critical bg-critical/5 text-critical",
 68 |               YELLOW: "border-l-warning bg-warning/5 text-warning",
 69 |               GREEN: "border-l-success bg-success/5 text-success",
 70 |             };
 71 | 
 72 |             return (
 73 |               <button
 74 |                 key={patient.id}
 75 |                 onClick={() => setSelectedId(patient.id)}
 76 |                 className={`w-full text-left rounded-xl border-l-4 transition-all duration-300 relative group
 77 |                   ${isSelected ? 'bg-bg-elevated ring-1 ring-border-subtle' : 'hover:bg-bg-elevated/40'}
 78 |                   ${riskColors[patient.risk]}
 79 |                 `}
 80 |               >
 81 |                 <div className="p-4">
 82 |                   <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
 83 |                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
 84 |                       <span className="text-lg font-black font-mono">
 85 |                         {patient.bed}
 86 |                       </span>
 87 |                       <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-text-muted px-2 py-0.5 bg-bg-main rounded uppercase">
 88 |                         {patient.id}
 89 |                       </span>
 90 |                     </div>
 91 |                     <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-text-muted italic">
 92 |                       {patient.lastUpdate}
 93 |                     </span>
 94 |                   </div>
 95 | 
 96 |                   <div className="text-sm font-bold text-text-primary mb-1">
 97 |                     {patient.name}
 98 |                   </div>
 99 | 
100 |                   {/* ALERTS: The "Why" behind the risk */}
101 |                   <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-2">
102 |                     {patient.alerts.length > 0 ? (
103 |                       patient.alerts.map(alert => (
104 |                         <span key={alert} className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-current/10 border border-current/20">
105 |                           {alert}
106 |                         </span>
107 |                       ))
108 |                     ) : (
109 |                       <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-text-muted uppercase">Vitals Stable</span>
110 |                     )}
111 |                   </div>
112 |                 </div>
113 | 
114 |                 {/* Selection Indicator */}
115 |                 {isSelected && (
116 |                   <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2">
117 |                     <i className="bi bi-chevron-right text-text-muted"></i>
118 |                   </div>
119 |                 )}
120 |               </button>
121 |             );
122 |           })
123 |         )}
124 |       </div>
125 | 
126 |       {/* FOOTER: System Health */}
127 |       <div className="p-4 bg-bg-panel border-t border-border-subtle">
128 |         <div className="rounded-lg bg-bg-main p-3 flex items-center justify-between">
129 |           <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
130 |              <div className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-accent animate-pulse"></div>
131 |              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-text-secondary uppercase">Inference Engine</span>
132 |           </div>
133 |           <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-accent">98.2ms</span>
134 |         </div>
135 |       </div>
136 |     </aside>
137 |   );
138 | }
</content>

<file><path>FE/README.md</path>
<content lines="1-73">
 1 | # React + TypeScript + Vite
 2 | 
 3 | This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.
 4 | 
 5 | Currently, two official plugins are available:
 6 | 
 7 | - [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
 8 | - [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)
 9 | 
10 | ## React Compiler
11 | 
12 | The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).
13 | 
14 | ## Expanding the ESLint configuration
15 | 
16 | If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:
17 | 
18 | ```js
19 | export default defineConfig([
20 |   globalIgnores(['dist']),
21 |   {
22 |     files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
23 |     extends: [
24 |       // Other configs...
25 | 
26 |       // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
27 |       tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
28 |       // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
29 |       tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
30 |       // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
31 |       tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,
32 | 
33 |       // Other configs...
34 |     ],
35 |     languageOptions: {
36 |       parserOptions: {
37 |         project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
38 |         tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
39 |       },
40 |       // other options...
41 |     },
42 |   },
43 | ])
44 | ```
45 | 
46 | You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://github.com/Rel1cx/eslint-react/tree/main/packages/plugins/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://github.com/Rel1cx/eslint-react/tree/main/packages/plugins/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:
47 | 
48 | ```js
49 | // eslint.config.js
50 | import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
51 | import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'
52 | 
53 | export default defineConfig([
54 |   globalIgnores(['dist']),
55 |   {
56 |     files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
57 |     extends: [
58 |       // Other configs...
59 |       // Enable lint rules for React
60 |       reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
61 |       // Enable lint rules for React DOM
62 |       reactDom.configs.recommended,
63 |     ],
64 |     languageOptions: {
65 |       parserOptions: {
66 |         project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
67 |         tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
68 |       },
69 |       // other options...
70 |     },
71 |   },
72 | ])
73 | ```
</content>

  1 | /**
  2 |  * SBARBrief Component - The "Money View" Right Panel
  3 |  * 
  4 |  * A high-contrast medical dashboard that presents critical patient deterioration
  5 |  * insights in under 60 seconds. Designed for non-clinical judges with a "Police Report"
  6 |  * aesthetic—urgent, factual, and clear.
  7 |  */
  8 | 
  9 | import { useState } from 'react';
 10 | import type { SBARBriefProps, RiskLevel } from '../../types/sbar.types';
 11 | 
 12 | const riskConfig: Record<RiskLevel, { bg: string; text: string; glow: string; border: string }> = {
 13 |   RED: {
 14 |     bg: 'bg-critical',
 15 |     text: 'text-critical',
 16 |     glow: 'shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(239,68,68,0.4)]',
 17 |     border: 'border-critical'
 18 |   },
 19 |   YELLOW: {
 20 |     bg: 'bg-warning',
 21 |     text: 'text-warning',
 22 |     glow: 'shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(234,179,8,0.4)]',
 23 |     border: 'border-warning'
 24 |   },

 31 | };

 39 |       {/* 60-Second Header - Responsive padding */}
 40 |       <header className={`${config.bg} px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 border-b-4 ${config.border}`}>

 44 |             {data.risk} RISK

131 |         >
132 |           <div className="flex items-center gap-2 md:gap-3">
133 |             <svg className="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5 text-highlight" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
134 |               <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" />
135 |             </svg>
136 |             <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-text-primary">
137 |               Evidence Tray
138 |             </span>
139 |             <span className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-text-muted">
140 |               ({data.reasoning.length} data points)
141 |             </span>
142 |           </div>
143 |           <svg
144 |             className={`w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5 text-text-secondary transition-transform ${isEvidenceExpanded ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
145 |             fill="none"
146 |             stroke="currentColor"
147 |             viewBox="0 0 24 24"
148 |           >
149 |             <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
150 |           </svg>
151 |         </button>
152 | 
153 |         {/* Collapsible Content */}
154 |         <div
155 |           className={`overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out ${
156 |             isEvidenceExpanded ? 'max-h-96' : 'max-h-0'
157 |           }`}
158 |         >
159 |           <div className="px-4 md:px-6 pb-4 md:pb-6 pt-2 bg-bg-main/50">
160 |             <p className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-text-muted mb-3 md:mb-4 italic">
161 |               Raw data points cited by the AI inference engine. This proves the analysis is grounded in actual measurements, not hallucinations.
162 |             </p>
163 |             <ul className="space-y-2">
164 |               {data.reasoning.map((item, index) => (
165 |                 <li key={index} className="flex items-start gap-2 md:gap-3">
166 |                   <div className="mt-1.5 w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-highlight flex-shrink-0"></div>
167 |                   <span className="text-xs md:text-sm text-text-primary font-mono leading-relaxed">
168 |                     {item}
169 |                   </span>
170 |                 </li>
171 |               ))}
172 |             </ul>
173 |           </div>
174 |         </div>
175 |       </div>
176 |     </div>
177 |   );
178 | }
179 | 
180 | // Made with Bob
</content>

<file><path>FE/src/features/agent-reasoning/components/AgentReasoningStream.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-153">
  1 | // Agent Reasoning Stream Container - Main component orchestrating all agents
  2 | 
  3 | import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
  4 | import { AgentPanel } from './AgentPanel';
  5 | import { CoordinatorPanel } from './CoordinatorPanel';
  6 | import { useAgentStream } from '../hooks/useAgentStream';
  7 | import type { AgentConfig } from '../types/agent-stream.types';
  8 | 
  9 | interface AgentReasoningStreamProps {
 10 |   patientId: string;
 11 |   className?: string;
 12 | }
 13 | 
 14 | // Agent configurations
 15 | const AGENT_CONFIGS: AgentConfig[] = [
 16 |   {
 17 |     id: 'trend',
 18 |     name: 'Trend Agent',
 19 |     accentColor: '#3b82f6', // Blue
 20 |     icon: '📈'
 21 |   },
 22 |   {
 23 |     id: 'lab-conflict',
 24 |     name: 'Lab-Conflict Agent',
 25 |     accentColor: '#eab308', // Yellow
 26 |     icon: '⚠️'
 27 |   },
 28 |   {
 29 |     id: 'timebomb',
 30 |     name: 'Time Bomb Agent',
 31 |     accentColor: '#ef4444', // Red
 32 |     icon: '⏰'
 33 |   }
 34 | ];
 35 | 
 36 | export function AgentReasoningStream({ patientId, className = '' }: AgentReasoningStreamProps) {
 37 |   const [agentsEnabled, setAgentsEnabled] = useState(true);
 38 |   const [completedAgents, setCompletedAgents] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
 39 |   const [coordinatorEnabled, setCoordinatorEnabled] = useState(false);
 40 | 
 41 |   // Initialize agent streams
 42 |   const trendStream = useAgentStream({
 43 |     patientId,
 44 |     agentId: 'trend',
 45 |     enabled: agentsEnabled,
 46 |     onComplete: () => handleAgentComplete('trend')
 47 |   });
 48 | 
 49 |   const conflictStream = useAgentStream({
 50 |     patientId,
 51 |     agentId: 'lab-conflict',
 52 |     enabled: agentsEnabled,
 53 |     onComplete: () => handleAgentComplete('lab-conflict')
 54 |   });
 55 | 
 56 |   const timebombStream = useAgentStream({
 57 |     patientId,
 58 |     agentId: 'timebomb',
 59 |     enabled: agentsEnabled,
 60 |     onComplete: () => handleAgentComplete('timebomb')
 61 |   });
 62 | 
 63 |   const coordinatorStream = useAgentStream({
 64 |     patientId,
 65 |     agentId: 'coordinator',
 66 |     enabled: coordinatorEnabled,
 67 |     onComplete: () => {}
 68 |   });
 69 | 
 70 |   // Handle agent completion
 71 |   const handleAgentComplete = (agentId: string) => {
 72 |     setCompletedAgents(prev => new Set([...prev, agentId]));
 73 |   };
 74 | 
 75 |   // Enable coordinator when all agents complete
 76 |   useEffect(() => {
 77 |     if (completedAgents.size === 3 && !coordinatorEnabled) {
 78 |       // Small delay before starting coordinator for dramatic effect
 79 |       setTimeout(() => {
 80 |         setCoordinatorEnabled(true);
 81 |       }, 500);
 82 |     }
 83 |   }, [completedAgents.size, coordinatorEnabled]);
 84 | 
 85 |   const allAgentsComplete = completedAgents.size === 3;
 86 | 
 87 |   return (
 88 |     <div className={`space-y-4 md:space-y-6 ${className}`}>
 89 |       {/* Header */}
 90 |       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 91 |         <div>
 92 |           <h2 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold text-text-primary">
 93 |             Agent Reasoning Stream
 94 |           </h2>
 95 |           <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-text-secondary mt-1">
 96 |             Live multi-agent analysis for Patient {patientId}
 97 |           </p>
 98 |         </div>
 99 |         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
100 |           <div className="text-xs text-text-muted">
101 |             {completedAgents.size}/3 agents complete
102 |           </div>
103 |           {allAgentsComplete && (
104 |             <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
105 |               <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
106 |             </svg>
107 |           )}
108 |         </div>
109 |       </div>
110 | 
111 |       {/* Agent Panels Grid - Responsive Layout */}
112 |       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-6">
113 |         <AgentPanel
114 |           config={AGENT_CONFIGS[0]}
115 |           streamState={trendStream}
116 |           className="animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left duration-500"
117 |         />
118 |         <AgentPanel
119 |           config={AGENT_CONFIGS[1]}
120 |           streamState={conflictStream}
121 |           className="animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left duration-500 [animation-delay:100ms]"
122 |         />
123 |         <AgentPanel
124 |           config={AGENT_CONFIGS[2]}
125 |           streamState={timebombStream}
126 |           className="animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left duration-500 md:col-span-2 xl:col-span-1 [animation-delay:200ms]"
127 |         />
128 |       </div>
129 | 
130 |       {/* Coordinator Panel - Appears after all agents complete */}
131 |       {coordinatorEnabled && (
132 |         <CoordinatorPanel streamState={coordinatorStream} />
133 |       )}
134 | 
135 |       {/* Demo Info */}
136 |       <div className="bg-bg-elevated border border-border-subtle rounded-lg p-3 md:p-4">
137 |         <div className="flex items-start gap-2 md:gap-3">
138 |           <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-highlight flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
139 |             <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
140 |           </svg>
141 |           <div className="flex-1">
142 |             <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-text-secondary leading-relaxed">
143 |               <strong className="text-text-primary">Demo Mode:</strong> This component showcases parallel multi-agent execution with simulated streaming. 
144 |               Each agent analyzes different aspects of patient data simultaneously, then the Coordinator synthesizes their findings.
145 |             </p>
146 |           </div>
147 |         </div>
148 |       </div>
149 |     </div>
150 |   );
151 | }
152 | 
153 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>

  1 | @import "tailwindcss";
  2 | @import "tw-animate-css";
  3 | @import "shadcn/tailwind.css";
  4 | @import "@fontsource-variable/inter";
  5 | 
  6 | @custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));
  7 | 
  8 | @theme {
  9 |   /* Layout Colors */
 10 |   --color-bg-main: #0b0f14;
 11 |   --color-bg-panel: #11161c;
 12 |   --color-bg-elevated: #161c23;
 13 | 
 14 |   /* Brand & Accents */
 15 |   --color-primary: #1f2933;
 16 |   --color-primary-hover: #273444;
 17 |   --color-accent: #334155;
 18 |   --color-highlight: #94a3b8;
 19 | 
 20 |   /* Typography */
 21 |   --color-text-primary: #e6edf3;
 22 |   --color-text-secondary: #9aa4af;
 23 |   --color-text-muted: #6b7280;
 24 | 
 25 |   /* Borders & Shadows */
 26 |   --color-border-subtle: #1f2933;
 27 |   --shadow-medical: 0 10px 15px -3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4), 0 4px 6px -2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
 28 | 
 29 |   /* Status Colors (Critical for ICU apps) */
 30 |   --color-success: #22c55e;
 31 |   --color-warning: #eab308;
 32 |   --color-critical: #ef4444;
 33 | }
 34 | 
 35 | /* --- Base Styles --- */
 36 | @layer base {
 37 |   body {
 38 |     @apply antialiased bg-background text-foreground;
 39 |     font-family: "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif;
 40 |     margin: 0;
 41 |     height: 100vh;
 42 |     display: flex;
 43 |   }
 44 | 
 45 |   h1, h2, h3 {
 46 |     @apply font-semibold tracking-tight;
 47 |   }
 48 |   * {
 49 |     @apply border-border outline-ring/50;
 50 |   }
 51 |   html {
 52 |     @apply font-sans;
 53 |   }
 54 | }
 55 | 
 56 | /* --- Components --- */
 57 | @layer components {
 58 |   .sidebar {
 59 |     @apply w-60 bg-bg-panel text-text-secondary border-r border-border-subtle flex flex-col transition-all;
 60 |   }
 61 | 
 62 |   .sidebar nav a {
 63 |     @apply flex items-center px-4 py-3 transition-colors duration-200 border-l-2 border-transparent;
 64 |     color: var(--color-text-secondary);
 65 |   }
 66 | 
 67 |   .sidebar nav a:hover {
 68 |     @apply bg-bg-elevated text-text-primary border-accent;
 69 |   }
 70 | 
 71 |   .sidebar nav a.active {
 72 |     @apply bg-accent/20 text-text-primary border-accent;
 73 |   }
 74 | 
 75 |   .main-inner {
 76 |     @apply max-w-5xl w-full mx-auto bg-bg-panel rounded-xl p-6 border border-border-subtle shadow-medical self-center;
 77 |   }
 78 | 
 79 |   /* ICU Patient/Data Card */
 80 |   .card {
 81 |     @apply bg-bg-panel rounded-lg p-5 text-left border border-border-subtle transition-all duration-200;
 82 |     border-left: 4px solid var(--color-accent);
 83 |   }
 84 | 
 85 |   .card:hover {
 86 |     @apply bg-bg-elevated -translate-y-0.5 shadow-lg;
 87 |   }
 88 | 
 89 |   .card h3 { @apply text-sm uppercase tracking-wider text-text-secondary mb-1; }
 90 |   .card p { @apply text-xl font-medium text-text-primary; }
 91 | 
 92 |   /* Status Indicators */
 93 |   .status-pill {
 94 |     @apply px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold uppercase;
 95 |   }
 96 | }
 97 | 
 98 | /* --- Utilities & Specific Fixes --- */
 99 | .status-pending  { color: var(--color-warning); }
100 | .status-approved { color: var(--color-success); }
101 | .status-rejected { color: var(--color-critical); }
102 | 
103 | button {
104 |   @apply px-4 py-2 rounded-md font-medium transition-all active:scale-95 cursor-pointer;
105 |   background: var(--color-accent);
106 |   color: var(--color-text-primary);
107 | }
108 | 
109 | button:hover {
110 |   @apply brightness-125 shadow-md;
111 | }
112 | 
113 | @theme inline {
114 |   --font-heading: var(--font-sans);
115 |   --font-sans: 'Inter Variable', sans-serif;
116 |   --color-sidebar-ring: var(--sidebar-ring);
117 |   --color-sidebar-border: var(--sidebar-border);
118 |   --color-sidebar-accent-foreground: var(--sidebar-accent-foreground);
119 |   --color-sidebar-accent: var(--sidebar-accent);
120 |   --color-sidebar-primary-foreground: var(--sidebar-primary-foreground);
121 |   --color-sidebar-primary: var(--sidebar-primary);
122 |   --color-sidebar-foreground: var(--sidebar-foreground);

154 | }

<content>
# Strategic UX Redesign Plan: ICU Silent Deterioration Spotter

## Executive Summary

This document outlines a comprehensive UX redesign strategy for the ICU Silent Deterioration Spotter application, transforming the current interface into an elevated, visually cohesive, and fully responsive medical dashboard. The redesign adapts the Spotify-inspired dark theme design system to create a premium clinical command center that prioritizes clarity, urgency, and actionable insights.

---

## 1. Design Philosophy & Vision

### Core Principles

**Medical-Grade Clarity**
- Information hierarchy optimized for split-second clinical decisions
- High-contrast visual language that works in ICU lighting conditions
- Zero ambiguity in risk communication (RED/YELLOW/GREEN states)

**Dark Immersive Environment**
- Near-black backgrounds (`#0b0f14`, `#11161c`, `#161c23`) reduce eye strain during long shifts
- Content-first approach where patient data and alerts are the primary visual focus
- Subtle UI elements that recede into darkness, letting critical information glow

**Systematic Geometry**
- Pill-shaped buttons (500px-9999px radius) for touch-optimized interactions
- Circular status indicators (50% radius) for quick visual scanning
- Consistent 8px spacing system for predictable layouts

**Responsive Excellence**
- Mobile-first approach with progressive enhancement
- Breakpoint strategy: 425px → 768px → 1024px → 1280px+
- Fluid typography and spacing that adapts seamlessly

---

## 2. Current State Analysis

### Existing Strengths
✅ Dark theme foundation with medical color semantics (critical/warning/success)
✅ Responsive grid layouts with Tailwind CSS
✅ Component-based architecture with shadcn/ui library
✅ SBAR Brief "Money View" with clear risk communication
✅ Agent Reasoning Stream with multi-agent visualization

### Areas for Improvement
❌ Inconsistent spacing and visual rhythm across components
❌ Underutilized shadcn/ui component library (60+ components available)
❌ Mixed design patterns (some Spotify-inspired, some generic)
❌ Cluttered information density in some panels
❌ Limited use of elevation and depth to establish hierarchy
❌ Responsive behavior could be more refined at breakpoints

---

## 3. Design System Adaptation

### Color Palette Refinement

```css
/* Primary Surfaces - Spotify-Inspired Depth */
--bg-deepest: #0b0f14;        /* Base layer (Level 0) */
--bg-main: #11161c;           /* Primary surface (Level 1) */
--bg-panel: #161c23;          /* Elevated cards (Level 2) */
--bg-elevated: #1f2933;       /* Hover/active states (Level 3) */

/* Medical Accent Colors */
--accent-primary: #1ed760;    /* Spotify Green - Primary CTAs, active states */
--accent-blue: #539df5;       /* Info/announcement states */
--accent-highlight: #94a3b8;  /* Secondary highlights */

/* Status Colors (Critical for ICU) */
--status-critical: #ef4444;   /* RED risk level */
--status-warning: #eab308;    /* YELLOW risk level */
--status-success: #22c55e;    /* GREEN risk level */

/* Typography Hierarchy */
--text-primary: #e6edf3;      /* Primary text (white) */
--text-secondary: #9aa4af;    /* Secondary text (silver) */
--text-muted: #6b7280;        /* Muted labels */

/* Borders & Shadows */
--border-subtle: #1f2933;
--shadow-medical: 0 10px 15px -3px rgba(0,0,0,0.4), 0 4px 6px -2px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);
--shadow-heavy: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);  /* Spotify-style heavy shadow */
```

### Typography System

```css
/* Font Stack - Inter Variable with System Fallbacks */
--font-primary: 'Inter Variable', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;

/* Type Scale - Compact & Functional */
--text-xs: 0.75rem;      /* 12px - Metadata, badges */
--text-sm: 0.875rem;     /* 14px - Body, navigation */
--text-base: 1rem;       /* 16px - Primary content */
--text-lg: 1.125rem;     /* 18px - Section headings */
--text-xl: 1.25rem;      /* 20px - Card titles */
--text-2xl: 1.5rem;      /* 24px - Page titles */
--text-3xl: 1.875rem;    /* 30px - Hero elements */

/* Font Weights */
--weight-regular: 400;
--weight-medium: 500;
--weight-semibold: 600;
--weight-bold: 700;
--weight-black: 900;

/* Letter Spacing for Buttons (Spotify Style) */
--tracking-button: 0.1em;  /* Uppercase button labels */
```

### Component Geometry

```css
/* Border Radius Scale */
--radius-sm: 0.375rem;    /* 6px - Small elements */
--radius-md: 0.5rem;      /* 8px - Cards, panels */
--radius-lg: 0.75rem;     /* 12px - Large cards */
--radius-xl: 1rem;        /* 16px - Dialogs */
--radius-pill: 9999px;    /* Full pill - Buttons, badges */
--radius-circle: 50%;     /* Circular - Status indicators */

/* Spacing System (8px base) */
--space-1: 0.25rem;   /* 4px */
--space-2: 0.5rem;    /* 8px */
--space-3: 0.75rem;   /* 12px */
--space-4: 1rem;      /* 16px */
--space-6: 1.5rem;    /* 24px */
--space-8: 2rem;      /* 32px */
--space-12: 3rem;     /* 48px */
```

---

## 4. Component Redesign Strategy

### 4.1 Sidebar (Patient Cohort List)

**Current Issues:**
- Fixed width doesn't adapt well to different screen sizes
- Patient cards could use more visual hierarchy
- Risk indicators could be more prominent

**Redesign Approach:**

```typescript
// Enhanced Sidebar with Spotify-inspired geometry
<aside className="
  w-80 lg:w-96                    // Responsive width
  bg-bg-panel 
  border-r border-border-subtle 
  flex flex-col h-screen 
  shadow-heavy                     // Spotify-style heavy shadow
">
  {/* Header with pill-shaped status indicator */}
  <header className="p-6 pb-4">
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
      <h2 className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.2em] text-text-secondary uppercase">
        Unit: ICU-North
      </h2>
      {/* Circular live indicator */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-success/10 rounded-full">
        <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-success animate-pulse"></span>
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-success uppercase tracking-wide">Live</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-text-primary tracking-tight">
      COHORT <span className="text-text-muted font-light">ANALYSIS</span>
    </h1>
  </header>

  {/* Pill-shaped filter tabs */}
  <nav className="flex gap-2 px-6 pb-4">
    <button className="
      px-4 py-2 
      bg-accent-primary text-bg-deepest 
      rounded-full                        // Pill shape
      text-xs font-bold uppercase 
      tracking-button                     // Wide letter spacing
      transition-all hover:scale-105
    ">
      All ({patients.length})
    </button>
    <button className="
      px-4 py-2 
      bg-bg-elevated text-text-secondary 
      rounded-full 
      text-xs font-bold uppercase 
      tracking-button
      hover:bg-bg-elevated/80
    ">
      Critical (2)
    </button>
  </nav>

  {/* Patient cards with enhanced visual hierarchy */}
  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-2 space-y-3">
    {patients.map(patient => (
      <button className="
        w-full text-left 
        rounded-xl                        // Comfortable radius
        border-l-4                        // Risk indicator
        border-l-critical                 // Dynamic based on risk
        bg-bg-elevated/40 
        hover:bg-bg-elevated 
        hover:shadow-medical              // Elevation on hover
        transition-all duration-300
        p-4
      ">
        {/* Patient info with improved spacing */}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {/* Circular bed number */}
            <div className="
              w-10 h-10 
              rounded-full                // Circle
              bg-critical/20 
              flex items-center justify-center
            ">
              <span className="text-lg font-black font-mono text-critical">
                {patient.bed}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm font-bold text-text-primary">
                {patient.name}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-text-muted font-mono">
                {patient.id}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-text-muted italic">
            {patient.lastUpdate}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Pill-shaped alert badges */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {patient.alerts.map(alert => (
            <span className="
              px-3 py-1 
              bg-critical/10 
              border border-critical/20 
              rounded-full                // Pill shape
              text-xs font-bold uppercase 
              text-critical
            ">
              {alert}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </button>
    ))}
  </div>
</aside>
```

### 4.2 Dashboard Layout

**Current Issues:**
- Layout could be more fluid across breakpoints
- Stats cards lack visual impact
- Agent stream integration feels disconnected

**Redesign Approach:**

```typescript
// Enhanced Dashboard with improved responsive behavior
<div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row gap-6 h-full">
  
  {/* LEFT COLUMN: Overview & Analytics */}
  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-6">
    
    {/* Hero Header with Spotify-inspired styling */}
    <header className="
      bg-gradient-to-br from-bg-panel to-bg-elevated 
      border border-border-subtle 
      rounded-2xl                         // Larger radius for prominence
      p-8 
      shadow-heavy                        // Heavy shadow for depth
    ">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-text-primary mb-2">
            Clinical Command Center
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
            ICU Silent Deterioration Spotter • Unit North
          </p>
        </div>
        
        {/* Pill-shaped toggle button */}
        <button className="
          px-6 py-3 
          bg-accent-primary 
          hover:bg-accent-primary/90 
          rounded-full                    // Full pill
          text-sm font-bold uppercase 
          tracking-button
          text-bg-deepest
          transition-all 
          hover:scale-105 
          active:scale-95
          shadow-lg
        ">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <svg className="w-5 h-5" />
            Show Agents
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>

    {/* Stats Grid with enhanced cards */}
    <section className="
      grid 
      grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 
      gap-4
    ">
      {stats.map(stat => (
        <div className="
          bg-bg-panel 
          border border-border-subtle 
          border-l-4 border-l-accent-primary  // Accent stripe
          rounded-xl 
          p-6 
          hover:bg-bg-elevated 
          hover:-translate-y-1              // Lift on hover
          hover:shadow-medical 
          transition-all duration-300
        ">
          <h3 className="
            text-xs font-bold uppercase 
            tracking-widest 
            text-text-secondary 
            mb-3
          ">
            {stat.label}
          </h3>
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3">
            <p className="text-4xl font-black text-text-primary">
              {stat.value}
            </p>
            {/* Pill-shaped trend indicator */}
            <span className="
              px-2 py-1 
              bg-success/10 
              rounded-full 
              text-xs font-bold 
              text-success
            ">
              +12%
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>

    {/* Agent Reasoning Stream */}
    <section className="
      bg-bg-panel 
      border border-border-subtle 
      rounded-2xl 
      p-6 
      shadow-medical
    ">
      <AgentReasoningStream />
    </section>
  </div>

  {/* RIGHT COLUMN: SBAR Brief */}
  <aside className="
    w-full xl:w-[480px] 2xl:w-[520px] 
    shrink-0
  ">
    <div className="xl:sticky xl:top-6 h-auto xl:h-[calc(100vh-3rem)]">
      <SBARBrief />
    </div>
  </aside>
</div>
```

### 4.3 SBAR Brief Component

**Current Issues:**
- Good foundation but could use more Spotify-inspired polish
- Risk indicators could be more dramatic
- Evidence tray could be more visually integrated

**Redesign Approach:**

```typescript
// Enhanced SBAR Brief with premium styling
<div className="
  h-full flex flex-col 
  bg-bg-panel 
  border border-border-subtle 
  rounded-2xl 
  overflow-hidden 
  shadow-heavy                          // Spotify-style heavy shadow
">
  {/* Risk Header with dramatic styling */}
  <header className={`
    ${riskConfig[risk].bg} 
    px-8 py-6 
    border-b-4 ${riskConfig[risk].border}
    shadow-heavy                        // Additional depth
  `}>
    {/* Full-width status bar with pill shape */}
    <div className="
      flex items-center justify-between 
      mb-4
    ">
      <div className="
        text-3xl font-black 
        tracking-wider 
        ${riskConfig[risk].text}
      ">
        {risk} RISK
      </div>
      {/* Pill-shaped active badge */}
      <div className="
        px-4 py-2 
        rounded-full 
        text-xs font-bold uppercase 
        tracking-button
        bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-sm
      ">
        Active Alert
      </div>
    </div>

    {/* Patient metadata grid */}
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6 mt-6">
      <div>
        <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 opacity-70">
          Patient ID
        </div>
        <div className="text-2xl font-mono font-black">
          {patientId}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 opacity-70">
          ICU Stay
        </div>
        <div className="text-2xl font-mono font-black">
          {stayCount}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>

  {/* Inference Engine Questions */}
  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-8 space-y-8">
    
    {/* Trend Agent */}
    <section>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
        {/* Circular indicator */}
        <div className="
          w-2 h-2 
          rounded-full 
          bg-accent-blue
        "></div>
        <h3 className="
          text-sm font-bold uppercase 
          tracking-widest 
          text-accent-blue
        ">
          What's Changing?
        </h3>
      </div>
      <p className="text-base leading-relaxed text-text-primary">
        {trend}
      </p>
    </section>

    {/* Time Bomb - Elevated card with heavy shadow */}
    <section className="
      border-4 ${riskConfig[risk].border} 
      rounded-2xl 
      p-6 
      bg-gradient-to-br from-bg-elevated to-bg-panel
      shadow-heavy                      // Dramatic elevation
    ">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
        <svg className="w-7 h-7 ${riskConfig[risk].text}" />
        <h3 className="
          text-sm font-black uppercase 
          tracking-widest 
          ${riskConfig[risk].text}
        ">
          Next 2 Hours
        </h3>
      </div>
      <p className="text-lg leading-relaxed font-bold ${riskConfig[risk].text}">
        {timeBomb}
      </p>
    </section>
  </div>

  {/* Evidence Tray - Collapsible with smooth animation */}
  <div className="border-t border-border-subtle">
    <button className="
      w-full px-8 py-5 
      flex items-center justify-between 
      hover:bg-bg-elevated 
      transition-colors
    ">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-accent-highlight" />
        <span className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">
          Evidence Tray
        </span>
        {/* Pill-shaped count badge */}
        <span className="
          px-3 py-1 
          bg-accent-highlight/10 
          rounded-full 
          text-xs font-bold 
          text-accent-highlight
        ">
          {evidence.length} points
        </span>
      </div>
      <svg className="w-5 h-5 transition-transform" />
    </button>
  </div>
</div>
```

### 4.4 Agent Reasoning Stream

**Current Issues:**
- Agent panels could be more visually distinct
- Coordinator panel needs more prominence
- Status indicators could be more dynamic

**Redesign Approach:**

```typescript
// Enhanced Agent Panels with Spotify-inspired styling
<div className="space-y-8">
  
  {/* Header with progress indicator */}
  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
    <div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-text-primary mb-2">
        Agent Reasoning Stream
      </h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
        Live multi-agent analysis for Patient {patientId}
      </p>
    </div>
    
    {/* Circular progress indicator */}
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
      <div className="
        px-4 py-2 
        bg-bg-elevated 
        rounded-full 
        text-xs font-bold 
        text-text-secondary
      ">
        {completedAgents}/3 complete
      </div>
      {allComplete && (
        <div className="
          w-10 h-10 
          rounded-full 
          bg-success/20 
          flex items-center justify-center
        ">
          <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-success" />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  </div>

  {/* Agent Grid with staggered animation */}
  <div className="
    grid 
    grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 
    gap-6
  ">
    {agents.map((agent, index) => (
      <div 
        className="
          bg-bg-panel 
          border-2 border-border-subtle 
          rounded-2xl 
          p-6 
          hover:border-accent-highlight 
          transition-all duration-300
          animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom
        "
        style={{ animationDelay: `${index * 100}ms` }}
      >
        {/* Agent header with circular icon */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div 
            className="
              w-12 h-12 
              rounded-full 
              flex items-center justify-center
              text-2xl
            "
            style={{ backgroundColor: `${agent.color}20` }}
          >
            {agent.icon}
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text-primary">
              {agent.name}
            </h3>
            {/* Pill-shaped status */}
            <span className="
              inline-block mt-1 
              px-2 py-0.5 
              bg-accent-highlight/10 
              rounded-full 
              text-xs font-bold 
              text-accent-highlight
            ">
              {agent.status}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Streaming content */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {agent.thoughts.map((thought, i) => (
            <div 
              className="
                text-sm text-text-secondary 
                animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left
              "
              style={{ animationDelay: `${i * 50}ms` }}
            >
              {thought}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    ))}
  </div>

  {/* Coordinator Panel - Elevated prominence */}
  {coordinatorActive && (
    <div className="
      bg-gradient-to-br from-accent-primary/10 to-bg-panel 
      border-2 border-accent-primary 
      rounded-2xl 
      p-8 
      shadow-heavy
      animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-700
    ">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-6">
        <div className="
          w-16 h-16 
          rounded-full 
          bg-accent-primary/20 
          flex items-center justify-center
        ">
          <svg className="w-8 h-8 text-accent-primary" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-text-primary">
            Coordinator Agent
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
            Synthesizing multi-agent findings
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* Coordinator synthesis */}
      <div className="
        bg-bg-elevated 
        rounded-xl 
        p-6 
        border border-border-subtle
      ">
        <p className="text-base leading-relaxed text-text-primary">
          {coordinatorSynthesis}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )}
</div>
```

---

## 5. Responsive Design Strategy

### Breakpoint System

```css
/* Mobile First Approach */
/* Base: 320px - 424px (Mobile Small) */
.container {
  padding: 1rem;
  font-size: 0.875rem;
}

/* 425px - 767px (Mobile) */
@media (min-width: 425px) {
  .container {
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }
  .sidebar {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

/* 768px - 1023px (Tablet) */
@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .container {
    padding: 2rem;
  }
  .stats-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }
  .agent-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }
}

/* 1024px - 1279px (Desktop Small) */
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  .sidebar {
    width: 20rem;  /* 320px */
    position: fixed;
    left: 0;
    top: 0;
    height: 100vh;
  }
  .main-content {
    margin-left: 20rem;
  }
  .stats-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  }
}

/* 1280px+ (Desktop Large) */
@media (min-width: 1280px) {
  .sidebar {
    width: 24rem;  /* 384px */
  }
  .main-content {
    margin-left: 24rem;
  }
  .agent-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  }
  .sbar-panel {
    width: 30rem;  /* 480px */
  }
}

/* 1536px+ (2XL) */
@media (min-width: 1536px) {
  .container {
    max-width: 1600px;
    margin: 0 auto;
  }
  .sbar-panel {
    width: 32.5rem;  /* 520px */
  }
}
```

### Mobile Navigation Strategy

```typescript
// Mobile: Bottom navigation bar
<nav className="
  fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 
  bg-bg-panel 
  border-t border-border-subtle 
  p-4 
  flex justify-around 
  lg:hidden
  shadow-heavy
  z-50
">
  <button className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
    <svg className="w-6 h-6" />
    <span className="text-xs font-bold">Patients</span>
  </button>
  <button className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
    <svg className="w-6 h-6" />
    <span className="text-xs font-bold">Alerts</span>
  </button>
  <button className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
    <svg className="w-6 h-6" />
    <span className="text-xs font-bold">Agents</span>
  </button>
</nav>

// Tablet/Desktop: Persistent sidebar
<aside className="
  hidden lg:flex 
  w-80 xl:w-96 
  fixed left-0 top-0 
  h-screen
">
  {/* Sidebar content */}
</aside>
```

## 6. Component Library Utilization

### Recommended shadcn/ui Components

**High Priority (Immediate Use)**
1. **Card** - Enhanced patient cards, stat cards, agent panels
2. **Badge** - Risk levels, status indicators, alert counts
3. **Button** - CTAs, navigation, actions (with pill styling)
4. **Separator** - Visual dividers between sections
5. **Tooltip** - Contextual help for medical terminology
6. **Dialog** - Patient detail modals, settings
7. **Sheet** - Mobile sidebar drawer
8. **Tabs** - Filter tabs, view switching
9. **Collapsible** - Evidence tray, expandable sections
10. **Progress** - Agent completion, data loading

**Medium Priority (Phase 2)**
11. **Dropdown Menu** - User actions, patient actions
12. **Popover** - Quick info, contextual actions
13. **Alert** - System notifications, warnings
14. **Skeleton** - Loading states
15. **Scroll Area** - Patient list, long content
16. **Avatar** - User profile, patient photos
17. **Switch** - Settings toggles
18. **Slider** - Time range selection

**Low Priority (Future Enhancement)**
19. **Calendar** - Date selection for historical data
20. **Chart** - Vitals visualization (already using Recharts)
21. **Command** - Quick search/navigation (Cmd+K)
22. **Context Menu** - Right-click actions
23. **Drawer** - Alternative to Sheet for mobile

### Component Customization Strategy

```typescript
// Example: Customized Button with Spotify styling
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

export function PillButton({ children, variant = "default", ...props }) {
  return (
    <Button
      className={cn(
        "rounded-full",              // Pill shape
        "px-6 py-3",                 // Comfortable padding
        "text-sm font-bold uppercase",
        "tracking-[0.1em]",          // Wide letter spacing
        "transition-all duration-300",
        "hover:scale-105",           // Subtle scale on hover
        "active:scale-95",           // Press effect
        "shadow-lg",                 // Elevation
        variant === "primary" && "bg-accent-primary text-bg-deepest hover:bg-accent-primary/90",
        variant === "secondary" && "bg-bg-elevated text-text-primary hover:bg-bg-elevated/80"
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </Button>
  )
}

// Example: Customized Card with medical styling
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"

export function MedicalCard({ risk, children, ...props }) {
  const riskColors = {
    critical: "border-l-critical",
    warning: "border-l-warning",
    success: "border-l-success",
    default: "border-l-accent-primary"
  }

  return (
    <Card
      className={cn(
        "bg-bg-panel",
        "border border-border-subtle",
        "border-l-4",
        riskColors[risk || "default"],
        "rounded-xl",
        "p-6",
        "hover:bg-bg-elevated",
        "hover:-translate-y-1",
        "hover:shadow-medical",
        "transition-all duration-300"
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </Card>
  )
}
```

## 8. Best Practices & Guidelines

### Code Organization

```
FE/src/
├── components/
│   ├── ui/                    # shadcn/ui base components
│   ├── medical/               # Custom medical components
│   │   ├── PillButton.tsx
│   │   ├── MedicalCard.tsx
│   │   ├── RiskBadge.tsx
│   │   ├── StatusIndicator.tsx
│   │   └── index.ts
│   └── layout/
│       ├── Sidebar.tsx
│       ├── Header.tsx
│       ├── Container.tsx
│       └── index.ts
├── features/
│   ├── dashboard/
│   ├── agent-reasoning/
│   └── components/
├── styles/
│   ├── index.css            # Design tokens
│   ├── animations.css       # Custom animations
│   └── utilities.css        # Utility classes
└── lib/
    ├── utils.ts
    └── constants.ts
```

### Naming Conventions

```typescript
// Component naming: PascalCase
export function PillButton() {}
export function MedicalCard() {}

// Props naming: camelCase
interface ButtonProps {
  variant: "primary" | "secondary"
  isLoading: boolean
  onClick: () => void
}

// CSS classes: kebab-case with BEM-inspired structure
.medical-card {}
.medical-card__header {}
.medical-card__content {}
.medical-card--critical {}

// Tailwind utilities: Use semantic names
className="bg-bg-panel text-text-primary border-border-subtle"
```

### Performance Optimization

```typescript
// 1. Lazy load heavy components
const AgentReasoningStream = lazy(() => import("@/features/agent-reasoning"))

// 2. Memoize expensive computations
const sortedPatients = useMemo(() => {
  return patients.sort((a, b) => riskOrder[a.risk] - riskOrder[b.risk])
}, [patients])

// 3. Debounce user inputs
const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(searchTerm, 300)

// 4. Virtualize long lists
import { useVirtualizer } from "@tanstack/react-virtual"

// 5. Optimize images
<img 
  src="/hero.png" 
  loading="lazy" 
  decoding="async"
  width={800}
  height={600}
/>
```

### Accessibility Guidelines

```typescript
// 1. Semantic HTML
<nav aria-label="Patient navigation">
  <button aria-pressed={isActive}>Filter</button>
</nav>

// 2. Keyboard navigation
<button
  onClick={handleClick}
  onKeyDown={(e) => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
      handleClick()
    }
  }}
>

// 3. Screen reader support
<div role="status" aria-live="polite">
  {completedAgents}/3 agents complete
</div>

// 4. Focus management
const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
useEffect(() => {
  if (isOpen) {
    buttonRef.current?.focus()
  }
}, [isOpen])

// 5. Color contrast (WCAG AA minimum)
// Ensure 4.5:1 contrast ratio for normal text
// Ensure 3:1 contrast ratio for large text (18px+)
```

## 9. Testing Strategy

### Visual Regression Testing

```typescript
// Using Playwright for visual testing
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test"

test("Dashboard matches design", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/dashboard")
  await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot("dashboard.png", {
    fullPage: true,
    threshold: 0.2
  })
})

test("Sidebar responsive behavior", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 375, height: 667 })
  await page.goto("/")
  await expect(page.locator(".sidebar")).toBeHidden()
  
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1024, height: 768 })
  await expect(page.locator(".sidebar")).toBeVisible()
})
```

### Component Testing

```typescript
// Using React Testing Library
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import { PillButton } from "@/components/medical/PillButton"

describe("PillButton", () => {
  it("renders with correct styling", () => {
    render(<PillButton>Click me</PillButton>)
    const button = screen.getByRole("button")
    expect(button).toHaveClass("rounded-full")
  })

  it("handles click events", async () => {
    const handleClick = jest.fn()
    render(<PillButton onClick={handleClick}>Click me</PillButton>)
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button"))
    expect(handleClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })
})
```

### Responsive Testing Matrix

| Device | Viewport | Test Scenarios |
|--------|----------|----------------|
| iPhone SE | 375x667 | Mobile navigation, touch interactions |
| iPhone 12 Pro | 390x844 | Mobile layout, gesture support |
| iPad | 768x1024 | Tablet grid, sidebar behavior |
| iPad Pro | 1024x1366 | Desktop-like experience |
| Desktop | 1280x720 | Full layout, all features |
| Large Desktop | 1920x1080 | Max-width container, spacing |

## 10. Success Metrics

### User Experience Metrics
- **Task Completion Time:** Reduce time to identify critical patients by 30%
- **Error Rate:** Decrease misinterpretation of risk levels by 50%
- **User Satisfaction:** Achieve 4.5/5 rating from clinical staff
- **Mobile Usability:** 90%+ task completion on mobile devices

### Technical Metrics
- **Page Load Time:** < 2 seconds on 3G connection
- **Time to Interactive:** < 3 seconds
- **Lighthouse Score:** 90+ for Performance, Accessibility, Best Practices
- **Bundle Size:** < 500KB initial load (gzipped)
- **Frame Rate:** Maintain 60fps during animations

### Design System Metrics
- **Component Reusability:** 80%+ of UI built from design system
- **Design Consistency:** 95%+ adherence to design tokens
- **Developer Velocity:** 50% faster component development
- **Maintenance Overhead:** 30% reduction in CSS codebase

---

## 11. Risk Mitigation

### Potential Challenges

**Challenge 1: Performance with Heavy Animations**
- **Risk:** Animations may cause jank on lower-end devices
- **Mitigation:** Use CSS transforms (GPU-accelerated), implement `prefers-reduced-motion`, lazy load animations

**Challenge 2: Responsive Complexity**
- **Risk:** Layout may break at edge-case viewport sizes
- **Mitigation:** Comprehensive responsive testing, fluid typography, container queries

**Challenge 3: Component Library Conflicts**
- **Risk:** shadcn/ui updates may break customizations
- **Mitigation:** Lock versions, maintain custom component layer, thorough testing

**Challenge 4: Accessibility Compliance**
- **Risk:** Complex interactions may not be keyboard-accessible
- **Mitigation:** ARIA labels, keyboard event handlers, screen reader testing

**Challenge 5: Browser Compatibility**
- **Risk:** Modern CSS features may not work in older browsers
- **Mitigation:** Progressive enhancement, feature detection, polyfills

---

## 12. Conclusion

This strategic UX redesign plan transforms the ICU Silent Deterioration Spotter into a premium, Spotify-inspired medical dashboard that prioritizes clarity, urgency, and actionable insights. By leveraging the existing shadcn/ui component library, implementing a systematic design language, and following best practices for responsive design and accessibility, we create an elevated user experience that empowers clinical staff to make split-second decisions with confidence.

The phased implementation approach ensures steady progress while maintaining system stability, and the comprehensive testing strategy guarantees quality across all devices and browsers. The result is a visually stunning, highly functional clinical command center that sets a new standard for medical dashboard design.

@import "tailwindcss";
@import "tw-animate-css";
@import "shadcn/tailwind.css";
@import "@fontsource-variable/inter";

@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));

@theme {
  /* Primary Surfaces - Spotify-Inspired Depth */
  --color-bg-deepest: #0b0f14;
  --color-bg-main: #11161c;
  --color-bg-panel: #161c23;
  --color-bg-elevated: #1f2933;

  /* Medical Accent Colors */
  --color-accent-primary: #1ed760;
  --color-accent-blue: #539df5;
  --color-accent-highlight: #94a3b8;

  /* Legacy Support (for gradual migration) */
  --color-primary: #1f2933;
  --color-primary-hover: #273444;
  --color-accent: #334155;
  --color-highlight: #94a3b8;

  /* Typography Hierarchy */
  --color-text-primary: #e6edf3;
  --color-text-secondary: #9aa4af;
  --color-text-muted: #6b7280;

  /* Borders & Shadows */
  --color-border-subtle: #1f2933;
  --shadow-medical: 0 10px 15px -3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4), 0 4px 6px -2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
  --shadow-heavy: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  --shadow-pill: 0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);

  /* Status Colors (Critical for ICU apps) */
  --color-success: #22c55e;
  --color-warning: #eab308;
  --color-critical: #ef4444;

  /* Border Radius Scale */
  --radius-sm: 0.375rem;
  --radius-md: 0.5rem;
  --radius-lg: 0.75rem;
  --radius-xl: 1rem;
  --radius-2xl: 1.5rem;
  --radius-pill: 9999px;
  --radius-circle: 50%;

  /* Spacing System (8px base) */
  --space-1: 0.25rem;
  --space-2: 0.5rem;
  --space-3: 0.75rem;
  --space-4: 1rem;
  --space-5: 1.25rem;
  --space-6: 1.5rem;
  --space-8: 2rem;
  --space-10: 2.5rem;
  --space-12: 3rem;
  --space-16: 4rem;

  /* Typography Scale */
  --text-xs: 0.75rem;
  --text-sm: 0.875rem;
  --text-base: 1rem;
  --text-lg: 1.125rem;
  --text-xl: 1.25rem;
  --text-2xl: 1.5rem;
  --text-3xl: 1.875rem;

  /* Font Weights */
  --weight-regular: 400;
  --weight-medium: 500;
  --weight-semibold: 600;
  --weight-bold: 700;
  --weight-black: 900;

  /* Letter Spacing */
  --tracking-tight: -0.025em;
  --tracking-normal: 0;
  --tracking-wide: 0.025em;
  --tracking-wider: 0.05em;
  --tracking-widest: 0.1em;
  --tracking-button: 0.1em;
}
